- **gos-eval**: Evaluates whether dependency-aware structural retrieval of agent skills improves task completion performance and computational efficiency compared to flat library access and semantic-only retrieval. It probes an agent's ability to assemble functionally complete, prerequisite-aware skill bundles for long-horizon technical and embodied sequential decision-making tasks. Use when the user wants to benchmark on SkillsBench, ALFWorld, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports average reward. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/gos-eval) · ★3)
- **gos-plan**: Use when breaking a spec into executable tasks — run gos instructions plan for step-by-step guidance, gos gate coverage to enforce Decision Coverage before exiting. ([chenw521/GOS](https://github.com/chenw521/GOS/tree/HEAD/skills/gos-plan) · ★3)
- **gos-project**: Use when starting a Lv4 multi-phase project or completing a milestone — manages PROJECT/ROADMAP/DECISIONS/REQUIREMENTS lifecycle, cross-phase context via rolling DECISIONS.md, drift check at transitions. ([chenw521/GOS](https://github.com/chenw521/GOS/tree/HEAD/skills/gos-project) · ★3)
- **gos-review**: Use when reviewing completed code — two-phase review (spec compliance first, then code quality). Run gos gate coverage to audit decisions. Never skip Phase 1. ([chenw521/GOS](https://github.com/chenw521/GOS/tree/HEAD/skills/gos-review) · ★3)
- **gos-router**: Use when starting any development task or resuming a GOS session — auto-activates to assess complexity, recommend level (Lv0~Lv4), enforce baseline ring (INTENT→VERIFY→TRACE), and chain stages. ([chenw521/GOS](https://github.com/chenw521/GOS/tree/HEAD/skills/gos-router) · ★3)
- **gos-ship**: Use when VERIFY passes — archive phase artifacts with delta.md, write change record with D-NN/R-NN traceability, run cleanup checklist, atomic commit. Every task leaves a trace. ([chenw521/GOS](https://github.com/chenw521/GOS/tree/HEAD/skills/gos-ship) · ★3)
- **gos-think**: Use when designing a feature or making decisions — interactive per-gray-area dialogue with one decision per message, D-NN decision tracking, and Observable Truths definition. Iron Law: no implementation before design approved. ([chenw521/GOS](https://github.com/chenw521/GOS/tree/HEAD/skills/gos-think) · ★3)
- **gos-verify**: Use before claiming any task complete — evidence-based one-truth-at-a-time verification. Run gos gate all (exit 0 required). Classify gaps for Gap Closure. Iron Law: evidence before assertions. ([chenw521/GOS](https://github.com/chenw521/GOS/tree/HEAD/skills/gos-verify) · ★3)
- **gosec**: Go security checker ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/gosec/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **gosec**: Run gosec, the Go security specialist. ([cricsion/cerberus](https://github.com/cricsion/cerberus/tree/HEAD/skills/gosec))
- **goship-deploy**: Deploy or port a project to GoShip (goship.sh). - **goshuin-travel-planner**: Discover public Goshuin Atlas temple, shrine, and goshuin catalogue records and plan culturally respectful visits in Japan. '기획보고서', '보고서 작성', '정책보고서', '사업계획서', '기안', '추진계획', '개선방안 보고서', '현황분석 보고서', '문제점 분석', '세부추진계획 수립', '대안 비교 분석' 등의 키워드 시 반드시 사용. SMART 목표 설정, MUST/WANT 의사결정 프레임워크, MECE/로직트리, 스토리텔링 6단계, 청와대 비서실 보고서 작성법을 적용. 파일이 여러 개일 경우 파일 간 연결고리를 파악하여 통합 분석하고, 연결고리가 없는 자료는 보고서에서 제외한다. 정량적 근거와 시각화 자료를 반드시 포함하며, 거짓·추측·가공 데이터를 절대 사용하지 않는다. 기본 문체는 모드 B 하이브리드(분석·논증·맥락 설명은 서술형, 수치·과제 목록·표는 개조식)로 작성하여 결재자가 맥락을 쉽게 파악할 수 있도록 한다. 사용자가 '개조식으로', '기안용', '공문 형식' 등을 명시 요청한 경우에만 모드 A 순수 개조식을 적… ([commetee12-source/gov-report-skill](https://github.com/commetee12-source/gov-report-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★3)
